# Graph Report - workspace  (2026-10-30)

## Corpus Check
- 1838 files · ~3,489,795 words
- Verdict: corpus is large enough that graph structure adds value.

## Summary
- 28483 nodes · 72919 edges · 1140 communities (980 shown, 160 thin omitted)
- Extraction: 93% EXTRACTED · 7% INFERRED · 0% AMBIGUOUS · INFERRED: 5412 edges (avg confidence: 0.8)
- Token cost: 0 input · 0 output

## Graph Freshness
- Built from commit: `a8fbda3c`
- Run `git rev-parse HEAD` and compare to check if the graph is stale.
- Run `graphify update .` after code changes (no API cost).
